## 66. Stage 67 16-Scenario Bilingual 7-Card 4:5 Benchmark Suite (`TO-01..TO-16` / Stage 67 16 场景中英双语 7-Card 4:5 基准评测)

- **English**: `lab/ucp_token_context_cart_offer_acp_telemetry_ap2_instrument_tau2_abort_bench.py` evaluates 16 deterministic scenarios (`TO-01..TO-16`) across Stage 66 protocol guards:
  - `TO-01..TO-04`: UCP `#793` permalink `ucp/version=YYYY-MM-DD` control parameter & redirect consumption, `#792` `TokenCredential` `binding`/`identity` context preservation, `#773` state-bound Cart `Offer` in `actions` map (`applies_to.cart_id` + `applies_to.state_ref`), `#740` immutable terminal Cart `status` (`ordered`/`canceled`/`expired`), `#730` `action.ready` `maxProperties: 0` (`{}`), `#719` `com.mercadopago.shopping.digital_delivery` Order pointer (`reference` + `lookup`), and `#716` OpenAPI 3.1 `components.securitySchemes`.
  - `TO-05..TO-08`: ACP `#238` `session_context` telemetry object vs. `delegate_payment.risk_signals` array, `#237` priority-ordered `app_links` with canonical `Product.url` fallback, `#232` post-checkout 3-field Order quantity conservation (`ordered == fulfilled + canceled`) & `completed` status, and `#149`/`#114`/`#111` `content_attribution`, `dev.acp.seller_backed.*` points balance check, and `x402` crypto facilitator settlement.
  - `TO-09..TO-12`: Google AP2 `#301` `AllowedPaymentInstrumentEvaluator` `(type, id)` tuple matching (`Fixes #299` item 2), `#307` versioned `delegate-SD-JWT` golden vector wire invariants (`N - 1` `~~` joins and trailing `~`), `#305` `@ap2-protocol/sdk` TypeScript/Zod receipt `oneOf` branch required fields, and `#216` `ghost-protocol` Base `8453` EIP-712 `IntentMandate` monthly cap.
  - `TO-13..TO-16`: Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 `#548` `###OUT-OF-SCOPE###` abort classification as `TerminationReason.OUT_OF_SCOPE` (`0.0` reward), `#562` fenced Markdown JSON extraction in NL-assertion judges, `#533` deferred checkpoint unlink on replacement write, and `#606`/`#602`/`#596` 97-task banking aggregate sync (task `102` `NL_ASSERTION`), lowercase airline `user_id`, and `campus` `v1.0.1` scoring.
  - **Headline Finding**: AI-Native deterministic guards achieve **16/16 (100.0%)** accuracy and **100.0%** order-reversal stability vs. **4/16 (25.0%)** for the Classic 3-Tier baseline, with median latency **0.34 ms** vs. **4.63 ms** (**13.6x** speedup), exported to `reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-token-context-cart-offer-acp-telemetry-ap2-instrument-tau2-abort-bench/{report.json,index.html,index.en.html}`.
- **中文**: `lab/ucp_token_context_cart_offer_acp_telemetry_ap2_instrument_tau2_abort_bench.py` 构建覆盖 Stage 66 四大护栏域的 16 个确定性评测场景（`TO-01..TO-16`）：AI-Native 护栏实现 **16/16 (100.0%)** 准确率与 **100.0%** 逆序扰动稳定性（对比经典三层架构基线 **4/16, 25.0%**），中位延迟 **0.34 ms vs. 4.63 ms（13.6x 加速）**，并生成中英双语自包含 7-Card 4:5 视觉评测报告 [`index.html`（中文）](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-token-context-cart-offer-acp-telemetry-ap2-instrument-tau2-abort-bench/index.html) 与 [`index.en.html`（English）](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-token-context-cart-offer-acp-telemetry-ap2-instrument-tau2-abort-bench/index.en.html)。

## 67. Stage 68 UCP OpenRPC Auth & Discount Preview / Generic SPT / Price Treatment / Event Feed, ACP Feed Upsert & Suggested Price / Marketing Consent / Razorpay & Cart, AP2 HNP Mandate Binding / String Amount / Budget / FCB, and Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 Spoken Amount & Action Matching Guards (`lab/ucp_openrpc_auth_acp_feed_consent_ap2_hnp_fcb_tau2_voice_guard.py`)

### 67.1 UCP OpenRPC `ec.auth`/`ep.cart.auth` Required `type` (`#802`), Catalog Discount Previews (`#715`), Generic SPT Handler (`#713`), `price_treatment.tax.inclusion` (`#691`), `event_feed`/`agent_hints` (`#673`) & Non-Empty `actions`/`policies.applies_to` (`#669`, `#661`)
- **English**: Enforces UCP PR `#802` (`Fixes #787`: `ec.auth` and `ep.cart.auth` OpenRPC descriptors MUST mark `params[name=="type"]` `"required": true`, and runtime embedded auth requests MUST carry a non-empty `type`, blocking `ucp_embedded_auth_type_param_not_required` and `ucp_embedded_auth_request_missing_type`), PR `#715` (`#707`: `dev.ucp.shopping.discount` extends `discounts.available[]` previews strictly to `get_product` Catalog Lookup responses while keeping Cart and Checkout authoritative for discount eligibility and application, blocking `ucp_catalog_discount_applied_forbidden_on_product` and `ucp_catalog_discount_available_on_non_get_product_op`), PR `#713` (`dev.ucp.payment.shared_payment_token` requires opaque, short-lived, merchant-scoped credentials without raw PAN/CVC/network token leakage `ucp_spt_handler_raw_pan_or_cvc_leaked`), PR `#691` (`#256`: `price.price_treatment.tax.inclusion` in `included | excluded | not_applicable` with unrecognized values normalized as omitted `tax` and blocking double-taxation on `included` prices `ucp_price_treatment_included_double_taxed`), PR `#673` (`event_feed` HTTPS `url` + non-empty `event_types[]` + transport in `webhook | sse | poll` and non-normative `fulfillment_event.agent_hints[]` requiring `action` with `additionalProperties: false` and forbidding unapproved financial auto-mutation `ucp_agent_hint_treated_as_mandatory_mutation`), and PR `#669`/`#661` (`actions` map `minProperties: 1` and `policy.applies_to` `minItems: 1` with product tag filter `$.products[?@.tags[?@=='...']]`).
- **中文**: 强制执行 UCP PR `#802`（修复 `#787`：OpenRPC `ec.auth` 与 `ep.cart.auth` 描述符中 `type` 参数必须声明 `"required": true` 且运行时请求必须携带非空 `type`，阻断 `ucp_embedded_auth_type_param_not_required`）、PR `#715`（`dev.ucp.shopping.discount` 仅在目录 `get_product` 响应中开放 `discounts.available[]` 预览并保持 Cart/Checkout 对折扣核销的唯一权威，阻断目录响应携带 `discounts.applied[]` `ucp_catalog_discount_applied_forbidden_on_product`）、PR `#713`（`dev.ucp.payment.shared_payment_token` 通用共享支付令牌要求短生命周期与商户作用域绑定并严禁泄露原始 PAN/CVC `ucp_spt_handler_raw_pan_or_cvc_leaked`）、PR `#691`（`price_treatment.tax.inclusion` 区分含税/不含税/不适用并阻断对 `included` 含税价重复加税 `ucp_price_treatment_included_double_taxed`）、PR `#673`（业务画像 `event_feed` 与履约事件非规范性建议 `agent_hints[]` 严禁越权自动执行财务变更 `ucp_agent_hint_treated_as_mandatory_mutation`）以及 PR `#669`/`#661`（`actions` 映射 `minProperties: 1` 非空约束与 `policy.applies_to` `minItems: 1` 及 `$.products[?@.tags[?@=='...']]` 集合匹配路径）。

### 67.2 ACP `UpsertProductsResponse` (`#255`), Feed API & Promotions (`#190`, `#192`), `Item.suggested_price` (`#201`), Complete `fulfillment_details` (`#200`), Marketing Consent (`#199`) & Razorpay / Pre-Checkout Cart (`#215`, `#213`, `#188`)
- **English**: Enforces ACP PR `#255`/`#190`/`#192` (`UpsertProductsResponse` strict `{"id": str, "accepted": bool}` with `additionalProperties: false` and Feed Promotions `effective_period.start_time <= end_time` + non-empty `benefits`/`applies_to`), PR `#201` (`SEP #197`: `Item.suggested_price` on `CheckoutSessionCreateRequest` requiring `PriceSource.feed_id` provenance and `observed_at`, blocking `acp_suggested_price_missing_feed_id_provenance`), PR `#200` (`SEP #196`: `CheckoutSessionCompleteRequest.fulfillment_details` MUST be applied by the seller BEFORE processing payment `acp_complete_fulfillment_details_applied_after_payment`), PR `#199` (`SEP #195`: `marketing_consent_options[]` HTTPS `privacy_policy_url` and `marketing_consents[]` channel membership check `acp_marketing_consent_undeclared_channel_or_missing_privacy_url`), and PR `#215`/`#213`/`#188` (`razorpay` PSP `in.razorpay.upi` `requires_delegate_payment: true` + `requires_pci_compliance: false` and pre-checkout `Cart` capability without premature payment handler negotiation `acp_cart_premature_payment_negotiation`).
- **中文**: 强制执行 ACP PR `#255`/`#190`/`#192`（`UpsertProductsResponse` 严格 `{"id", "accepted"}` 结构与推送式商品/促销流时间窗校验）、PR `#201`（`SEP #197`：创建结账会话 `Item.suggested_price` 必须包含 `PriceSource.feed_id` 来源溯源与 `observed_at`）、PR `#200`（`SEP #196`：结账完成请求携带 `fulfillment_details` 时卖家必须在扣款前先应用联系信息更新 `acp_complete_fulfillment_details_applied_after_payment`）、PR `#199`（`SEP #195`：营销授权选项 HTTPS 隐私政策链接与提交渠道白名单校验）以及 PR `#215`/`#213`/`#188`（`razorpay` PSP 与 `in.razorpay.upi` 委托支付/免 PCI 属性及结账前 `Cart` 能力禁止提前触发支付处理器协商 `acp_cart_premature_payment_negotiation`）。

### 67.3 Google AP2 HNP Mandate Security Binding (`#202`, `#189`), W3C `DOMString` Currency Value (`#191`), `IntentMandate.budget` Ceiling (`#198`), Section 7.4 Fiduciary Circuit Breaker (`#187`, `#164`) & UPI Collect (`#139`)
- **English**: Enforces Google AP2 PR `#191` (`PaymentCurrencyAmount.value` MUST be a `str` `DOMString` per W3C Payment Request API spec rather than `float`/`int` to prevent IEEE-754 precision drift `ap2_payment_currency_amount_value_not_string`), PR `#198` (`Closes #133`: `IntentMandate.budget` spend ceiling enforced against `PaymentMandate.total_amount`, blocking `ap2_intent_mandate_budget_exceeded`), PR `#202`/`#189` (in `human_not_present` modality with `user_cart_confirmation_required == False`, `IntentMandate` requires `id` and 3-segment `user_authorization` JWT, and `PaymentMandate` requires matching `intent_mandate_id` and `signed_intent_mandate_hash`, blocking `ap2_hnp_intent_missing_user_authorization` and `ap2_hnp_payment_missing_intent_mandate_binding`), PR `#187`/`#164` (Section 7.4 `RiskPayload` and Fiduciary Circuit Breaker `FCBState.OPEN` or `TRIPPED` condition blocking autonomous execution `ap2_fcb_circuit_open_or_condition_tripped`), and PR `#139` (UPI Collect 3-section request envelope `transaction_data`, `cred_block`, `device_fingerprint`).
- **中文**: 强制执行 Google AP2 PR `#191`（`PaymentCurrencyAmount.value` 必须对齐 W3C Payment Request API `DOMString` 字符串类型，阻断浮点数传入 `ap2_payment_currency_amount_value_not_string`）、PR `#198`（`Closes #133`：`IntentMandate.budget` 机器可读支出上限强校验 `ap2_intent_mandate_budget_exceeded`）、PR `#202`/`#189`（HNP 用户不在场模式下强制校验 `IntentMandate.user_authorization` 用户签名 JWT 与 `PaymentMandate` 的 `intent_mandate_id` + `intent_mandate_hash` 跨授权书哈希绑定）、PR `#187`/`#164`（Section 7.4 结构化风险信号 `RiskPayload` 与受托熔断器 `FCBState.OPEN` / `TRIPPED` 实时阻断自主执行）及 PR `#139`（UPI Collect 三段式请求信封 `transaction_data`、`cred_block`、`device_fingerprint`）。

### 67.4 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 Spoken & Chunked Voice Amount Recognition (`#582`), Safe `analyze_reward` Lookup (`#575`), Trajectory `replay_mismatches` (`#573`), Union/Paired Action Matching (`#569`, `#570`) & Retail Reference Integrity (`#571`, `#567`, `#566`)
- **English**: Enforces Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#582` (`CommunicateEvaluator` and `FullDuplexCommunicateEvaluator` recognize amounts spoken as words like `"five thousand two hundred forty four"` $\rightarrow$ `"5244"` and reconstruct numbers split across streaming `Tick` chunks, blocking false negatives `tau2_voice_spoken_or_chunked_amount_false_negative`), PR `#575` (`analyze_reward()` uses safe `.get()` when `reward_basis` names an absent component in `reward_breakdown` instead of raising `KeyError` `tau2_analyze_reward_missing_component_keyerror`), PR `#573` (`Environment.set_state` returns `replay_mismatches` count recorded in `RewardInfo.info` and rejects divergent replays in strict mode `tau2_silent_lenient_replay_divergence`), PR `#569`/`#570` (`Action.compare_with_tool_call` compares the union of `tool_call.arguments.keys() | self.arguments.keys()` and sorts paired scalar lists like `item_ids`/`new_item_ids` row-by-row), and PR `#571`/`#567`/`#566` (retail task `105` gift-card balance check `$21.10 <= balance` and task `64` delivered-order exchange prerequisite `tau2_retail_reference_insufficient_gift_card_or_pending_exchange`).
- **中文**: 强制执行 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#582`（语音通信评估器支持识别英语口语金额如 `"five thousand two hundred forty four"` $\rightarrow$ `"5244"` 并重组跨流式分片数字，阻断假阴性判零 `tau2_voice_spoken_or_chunked_amount_false_negative`）、PR `#575`（`analyze_reward()` 对 `reward_basis` 声明但 `reward_breakdown` 缺失的组件使用 `.get()` 安全读取，消除 `KeyError` 崩溃）、PR `#573`（`evaluate-trajs` 显式统计并上报 `replay_mismatches` 轨迹重放分歧数并支持 `--strict-replay`）、PR `#569`/`#570`（`Action.compare_with_tool_call` 比较双方参数键并集并对同长度标量列表如 `item_ids`/`new_item_ids` 按行配对排序）及 PR `#571`/`#567`/`#566`（零售域参考动作礼品卡余额充足性与 `delivered` 已交付订单换货前置校验）。

## 68. Stage 69 16-Scenario Bilingual 7-Card 4:5 Benchmark Suite (`OA-01..OA-16`: `lab/ucp_openrpc_auth_acp_feed_consent_ap2_hnp_fcb_tau2_voice_bench.py`)

- **English**: `lab/ucp_openrpc_auth_acp_feed_consent_ap2_hnp_fcb_tau2_voice_bench.py` evaluates 16 deterministic scenarios (`OA-01..OA-16`) across the four Stage 68 guardrail families:
  - `OA-01..OA-04`: UCP `#802` OpenRPC `ec.auth`/`ep.cart.auth` required `type` parameter, `#715` `get_product` `discounts.available[]` preview vs. forbidden `discounts.applied[]`, `#713` opaque `dev.ucp.payment.shared_payment_token` without raw PAN/CVC leakage, `#691` `price_treatment.tax.inclusion="included"` double-tax prevention, `#673` `event_feed` & non-normative `agent_hints[]`, and `#669`/`#661` non-empty `actions` & `policy.applies_to`.
  - `OA-05..OA-08`: ACP `#255`/`#190`/`#192` `UpsertProductsResponse` `additionalProperties: false` & Feed Promotions, `#201` `Item.suggested_price` `PriceSource.feed_id` provenance, `#200` `CheckoutSessionCompleteRequest.fulfillment_details` applied BEFORE payment processing, `#199` HTTPS `privacy_policy_url` & declared marketing consent channels, and `#215`/`#213`/`#188` `in.razorpay.upi` & pre-checkout `Cart` isolation.
  - `OA-09..OA-12`: Google AP2 `#191` `PaymentCurrencyAmount.value` `DOMString` string contract, `#198` `IntentMandate.budget` ceiling, `#202`/`#189` `human_not_present` `user_authorization` JWT & `signed_intent_mandate_hash` binding, `#187`/`#164` Section 7.4 `RiskPayload` & Fiduciary Circuit Breaker `FCBState.OPEN` / `TRIPPED` halt, and `#139` 3-section UPI Collect envelope.
  - `OA-13..OA-16`: Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 `#582` spoken word-amount `"five thousand two hundred forty four"` $\rightarrow$ `"5244"` & cross-tick chunk reconstruction, `#575` safe `.get()` lookup in `analyze_reward()`, `#573` `evaluate-trajs` `replay_mismatches` tracking, `#569`/`#570` `Action.compare_with_tool_call` union keys & row-by-row paired scalar list sorting, and `#571`/`#567`/`#566` retail gift-card balance & delivered-order exchange validation.
  - **Headline Finding**: AI-Native deterministic guards achieve **16/16 (100.0%)** accuracy and **100.0%** order-reversal stability vs. **4/16 (25.0%)** for the Classic 3-Tier baseline, with median latency **0.34 ms** vs. **4.63 ms** (**13.6x** speedup), exported to `reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-openrpc-auth-acp-feed-consent-ap2-hnp-fcb-tau2-voice-bench/{report.json,index.html,index.en.html}`.
- **中文**: `lab/ucp_openrpc_auth_acp_feed_consent_ap2_hnp_fcb_tau2_voice_bench.py` 构建覆盖 Stage 68 四大护栏域的 16 个确定性评测场景（`OA-01..OA-16`）：AI-Native 护栏实现 **16/16 (100.0%)** 准确率与 **100.0%** 逆序扰动稳定性（对比经典三层架构基线 **4/16, 25.0%**），中位延迟 **0.34 ms vs. 4.63 ms（13.6x 加速）**，并生成中英双语自包含 7-Card 4:5 视觉评测报告 [`index.html`（中文）](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-openrpc-auth-acp-feed-consent-ap2-hnp-fcb-tau2-voice-bench/index.html) 与 [`index.en.html`（English）](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-openrpc-auth-acp-feed-consent-ap2-hnp-fcb-tau2-voice-bench/index.en.html)。

## 69. Stage 70 UCP Webhook Signing Gate & `map_order` / `request_constraints` / Sale-Basis Quantities, ACP `protocol.extensions` & Line-Item `valid_until` / `Merchant-Signature` / 11 Session States / MCP Binding, Google AP2 `FailureResponse` & `PaymentReceipt` / Verifiable Intent / Micropayment Channels, and Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 Live Interruption & Result-Safe Retries / Pydantic Coercion / `pass^k` Metrics Guards (`lab/ucp_webhook_signing_acp_session_states_ap2_failure_micropay_tau2_solo_guard.py`)

### 69.1 UCP Order Webhook Signing Gate (`#659`), Reserved `ucp` Namespace & `map_order` (`#658`, `#657`), Response-Carried `request_constraints` (`#655`, `#626`) & Sale-Basis Step Quantities (`#653`, `#642`, `#622`, `#596`)
- **English**: Enforces UCP PR `#659` (`webhook-id` and `webhook-timestamp` join the closed RFC 9421 signing gate; `event_id` and `created_time` are forbidden inside the `order_event_webhook` JSON body `ucp_webhook_body_legacy_event_fields_present` / `ucp_webhook_signing_gate_missing_id_or_timestamp`), PR `#658`/`#657` (`ucp.capabilities` MUST be an object map with a `schema` URL per capability; reserves `ucp` at every structured object scope covered by AP2 `merchant_authorization` JCS and adds response-only `ucp.map_order` `ucp_map_order_or_constraints_forbidden_on_request`), PR `#655`/`#626` (`ucp.request_constraints` closed `required`/`properties`/`enum`/`const` grammar requiring an explicit RFC 9535 JSONPath `path` on available payment instruments `ucp_instrument_request_constraints_missing_path`), PR `#653` (integer step count `quantity` with `quantity_unit` `10^-scale` step arithmetic `ucp_quantity_sale_basis_invalid_step_or_scale`), and PRs `#642`/`#622`/`#596` (`context.location` precedence, MCP `-32004` `rate_limit_exceeded`, and `dev.ucp.user_id`/`dev.ucp.user_location` signals).
- **中文**: 强制执行 UCP PR `#659`（将 `webhook-id` 与 `webhook-timestamp` 纳入 RFC 9421 闭集必签头并严禁订单 Webhook JSON 正文残留 `event_id`/`created_time`）、PR `#658`/`#657`（`ucp.capabilities` 必须为携带 `schema` URL 的字典映射；在所有结构化对象作用域保留 `ucp` 命名空间并纳入 AP2 `merchant_authorization` JCS 签名覆盖，新增仅限响应携带的 `ucp.map_order`）、PR `#655`/`#626`（响应携带的 `ucp.request_constraints` 请求约束语法及可用支付工具必填 RFC 9535 JSONPath `path`）、PR `#653`（整数步数 `quantity` 与 `quantity_unit` `10^-scale` 销售基准算术）及 PR `#642`/`#622`/`#596`（`context.location` 优先级、MCP `-32004` 限流错误码与反滥用信号）。

### 69.2 ACP `protocol.extensions` Relocation (`#178`), Per-Line-Item `valid_until` Offer Expiry (`#165`), `Merchant-Signature` Replay Protection (`#160`), 11 Session States & Digital Order Status (`#144`, `#162`, `#161`) & MCP Transport Binding (`#139`, `#130`, `#125`)
- **English**: Enforces ACP PR `#178` (moving extension declarations from `capabilities.extensions` to `protocol.extensions` `acp_extensions_declared_under_capabilities_instead_of_protocol`), PR `#165` (`SEP #164`: per-line-item `valid_until` RFC 3339 timestamp and `availability_status: "offer_valid" | "offer_expired"` enforced independently of session `quote_expires_at` `acp_line_item_offer_valid_until_expired`), PR `#160` (Stripe-aligned `Merchant-Signature: t=<unix_seconds>,v1=<64_hex>` HMAC-SHA256 over `"{timestamp}.{raw_body}"` with 300s replay protection `acp_webhook_merchant_signature_invalid_or_replay_window_exceeded`), PRs `#144`/`#162`/`#161` (all 11 `CheckoutSessionBase.status` states, forbidding `shipped` on digital fulfillments `acp_digital_order_shipped_status_invalid`, and 401/500/503 delegate payment error enums), and PR `#139` (5 MCP checkout tools requiring `arguments.meta.api_version` and `arguments.payload`).
- **中文**: 强制执行 ACP PR `#178`（将扩展声明从 `capabilities.extensions` 迁移至 `protocol.extensions`）、PR `#165`（`SEP #164`：逐行商品限时报价 `valid_until` 与 `offer_valid`/`offer_expired` 状态独立于会话级 `quote_expires_at` 校验）、PR `#160`（Stripe 对齐的 `Merchant-Signature: t=<unix_seconds>,v1=<64_hex>` HMAC-SHA256 签名与 300 秒防重放窗口）、PR `#144`/`#162`/`#161`（完整 11 种结账会话状态、数字商品履约严禁标记为物理运输 `shipped`、以及委托支付 401/500/503 错误枚举）及 PR `#139`（5 个 MCP 结账工具绑定要求 `arguments.meta.api_version` 与 `arguments.payload`）。

### 69.3 Google AP2 `FailureResponse` Taxonomy (`#131`, `#132`), Section 7.1 `PaymentReceipt` & Mandate JSON Schemas (`#110`, `#128`, `#92`), Verifiable Intent 3-Hop Chain (`#144`) & Micropayment Channels (`#83`)
- **English**: Enforces Google AP2 PR `#131`/`#132` (`ap2.errors.FailureResponse` mapping `FailureCategory` to `RecoveryAction` and blocking silent autonomous retry when `USER_CONFIRMATION_REQUIRED` or `NEW_MANDATE_REQUIRED` `ap2_failure_recovery_autonomous_retry_forbidden`), PR `#110`/`#128`/`#92` (Section 7.1 `PaymentReceipt` null-safe `artifacts is None` handling, language-agnostic Mandate JSON Schema `$schema`/`$id`, and PCI/SOX/GDPR mandate audit log redaction), and PR `#144`/`#83` (fail-closed Verifiable Intent 3-hop `issuer_credential -> user_mandate -> agent_fulfillment` SD-JWT chain and AP2 Micropayment Channel `micropayment-provider` / `streaming-payment-consumer` cumulative deposit and monotonic `sequence_number` bounds `ap2_micropayment_channel_overdraft_or_nonce_replay`).
- **中文**: 强制执行 Google AP2 PR `#131`/`#132`（`ap2.errors.FailureResponse` 错误分类与恢复动作映射，在要求 `USER_CONFIRMATION_REQUIRED` 或 `NEW_MANDATE_REQUIRED` 时阻断智能体静默重试 `ap2_failure_recovery_autonomous_retry_forbidden`）、PR `#110`/`#128`/`#92`（Section 7.1 `PaymentReceipt` 对 `artifacts is None` 的空安全提取、跨语言 Mandate JSON Schema `$schema`/`$id` 校验与 PCI/SOX/GDPR 审计日志脱敏）以及 PR `#144`/`#83`（Verifiable Intent 三跳 SD-JWT 委托链故障闭合验签与微支付状态通道累计存款上限及单调递增序号防重放 `ap2_micropayment_channel_overdraft_or_nonce_replay`）。

### 69.4 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 OpenAI Live Interruption Signals (`#528`), Result-Safe Hallucination Retries (`#520`), Pydantic Tool Kwargs Coercion (`#516`, `#513`), `llm_agent_solo_gt` Replay (`#508`), `pass^k` Infra-Error Denominator (`#498`) & Fixed Closure Reasons (`#484`)
- **English**: Enforces Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#528` (`LiveInputTranscriptDelta` `session.input_transcript.delta` sets `speech_started=True` / `contains_speech=True` without falsely setting `was_truncated=True` `tau2_live_input_transcript_falsely_marked_truncated`), PR `#520` (result-safe full-duplex hallucination retries requiring `hallucinations` to be a `list`, reviewing the final retry with `review_model`, checkpointing only the selected run, and preserving complete tool results), PR `#516`/`#513` (runtime Pydantic coercion of float-encoded integer tool kwargs `5.0 -> 5` before DB persistence `tau2_tool_kwargs_uncoerced_float_db_hash_drift`), PR `#508` (recognizing `"llm_agent_solo_gt"` in `SOLO_AGENT_IMPLEMENTATIONS` so `TelecomUserTools` load during replay `tau2_solo_gt_missing_user_tools_on_replay`), PR `#498` (comparing `termination_reason` against `TerminationReason.INFRASTRUCTURE_ERROR.value` under pandas 3.x and keeping all-infra-error tasks in the `pass^k` denominator with `pass^k = 0` `tau2_pass_hat_k_infra_error_denominator_inflation`), and PR `#484` (restricting `close_bank_account_7392` `reason` to the fixed enum set `tau2_banking_close_account_freeform_reason_corrupts_db_hash`).
- **中文**: 强制执行 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#528`（OpenAI Live `session.input_transcript.delta` 映射为 `speech_started=True` 且不误置 `was_truncated=True`）、PR `#520`（全双工幻觉重试结果安全：校验 `hallucinations` 为列表、对最终重试执行评审、仅写入选中轨迹并保留完整工具结果）、PR `#516`/`#513`（在工具调用前通过 Pydantic 将浮点编码整数 `5.0 -> 5` 强制转换以防止黄金 DB 哈希漂移 `tau2_tool_kwargs_uncoerced_float_db_hash_drift`）、PR `#508`（重放评分时识别 `"llm_agent_solo_gt"` 为 Solo 模式以加载 `TelecomUserTools`）、PR `#498`（按 `.value` 过滤基础设施错误并在 `pass^k` 分母中保留全故障任务记 `0` 分 `tau2_pass_hat_k_infra_error_denominator_inflation`）以及 PR `#484`（`close_bank_account_7392` 关户原因固定枚举校验以防自由文本污染 DB 哈希）。

## 71. Stage 72: UCP `buyer.consent` Full-Replacement Exemption & Return Policy Extension / `ETag` Conditional Writes / `P-521` `ES512` Key Pairing (`#660`, `#634`, `#628`, `#627`, `#625`, `#617`, `#605`), ACP Recurring Allowance & Write-Only Affiliate Attribution / Intent Traces / 3DS `channel` Removal (`#85`, `#80`, `#73`, `#57`, `#54`, `#45`), Google AP2 `AP2-Haggle` Negotiation & Solana/Algorand Mandate Binding / Credential Sanitization (`#229`, `#228`, `#218`, `#212`, `#188`), and Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 Banking Read Allowlist & Multi-Item Variant Pricing / VPN `deepcopy` / Replay Hallucination No-Op (`#329`, `#322`, `#301`, `#273`, `#264`)

### 71.1 UCP `buyer.consent` Exemption (`#660`), `dev.ucp.shopping.policy.return` (`#634`), `ETag`/`If-Match` Conditional Writes (`#628`), Authority Binding (`#627`), `P-521` $\leftrightarrow$ `ES512` (`#625`) & Fulfillment/Inventory Signals (`#617`, `#605`)
- **English**: `verify_ucp_consent_persistence_return_policy_etag_p521_and_fulfillment_link` in `lab/ucp_consent_return_etag_acp_affiliate_trace_ap2_haggle_solana_tau2_vpn_guard.py` enforces UCP PR `#660` (`buyer.consent` is exempt from Cart and Checkout full-replacement update rules so omitting `buyer.consent` retains the buyer's prior consent/opt-out state `ucp_buyer_consent_dropped_on_full_replacement_update`), PR `#634` (`dev.ucp.shopping.policy.return` forbids `window`, `supported_resolutions`, `methods`, and `restocking_fee` when `final_sale == True` `ucp_return_policy_final_sale_with_return_terms_invalid`, requires both positive integer `days` and explicit `anchor` on `window` `ucp_return_policy_window_missing_days_or_anchor`, and forbids rendering omitted fees as free `ucp_return_policy_unstated_fee_claimed_as_free`), PR `#628` (RFC 9110 `ETag` / `If-Match` conditional writes rejecting stale `If-Match` with `412 Precondition Failed` and missing required `If-Match` with `428 Precondition Required` `ucp_checkout_conditional_write_etag_mismatch_or_missing`), PR `#627` & `#625` (exact `name == authority_prefix` or `name.startswith(authority_prefix + ".")` authority binding `ucp_authority_binding_host_mismatch` and `crv: "P-521"` paired exclusively with `alg: "ES512"` `ucp_profile_p521_key_alg_mismatch`), and PRs `#617`/`#605` (`fulfillment_event.estimated_delivery.earliest <= latest` and non-negative integer `availability.quantity` `ucp_fulfillment_event_or_inventory_signal_invalid`).
- **中文**: `lab/ucp_consent_return_etag_acp_affiliate_trace_ap2_haggle_solana_tau2_vpn_guard.py` 中的 `verify_ucp_consent_persistence_return_policy_etag_p521_and_fulfillment_link` 强制执行 UCP PR `#660`（`buyer.consent` 豁免于购物车与结账的全量替换规则，更新省略 `buyer.consent` 时必须保留买家既有同意/拒收状态 `ucp_buyer_consent_dropped_on_full_replacement_update`）、PR `#634`（`dev.ucp.shopping.policy.return` 在 `final_sale == True` 时禁止共现 `window`/`supported_resolutions`/`methods`/`restocking_fee` `ucp_return_policy_final_sale_with_return_terms_invalid`、`window` 必填正整数 `days` 与显式 `anchor`、禁止将未声明费用渲染为免费）、PR `#628`（RFC 9110 `ETag` / `If-Match` 条件写前置校验拦截过期或缺失 `If-Match` `ucp_checkout_conditional_write_etag_mismatch_or_missing`）、PR `#627`/`#625`（权威绑定兼容 `name == authority_prefix` 精确匹配且 `crv: "P-521"` 仅配对 `alg: "ES512"` `ucp_profile_p521_key_alg_mismatch`）以及 PR `#617`/`#605`（履约事件预计送达时间窗与非负整数库存信号校验）。

### 71.2 ACP 3DS `AuthenticationMetadata` `channel` Removal (`#85`), `authentication_provider` (`#80`), Recurring/Subscription `Allowance` (`#73`), Write-Only `AffiliateAttribution` (`#57`), Cancellation `IntentTrace` (`#54`) & Lodging Live Rate Hydration (`#45`)
- **English**: `verify_acp_recurring_allowance_affiliate_attribution_intent_trace_and_3ds_channel` enforces ACP PR `#85` & `#80` (removing deprecated `channel` from `AuthenticationMetadata` `acp_3ds_authentication_metadata_deprecated_channel_present` and requiring `PaymentProvider.merchant_id` `acp_payment_provider_missing_merchant_id_or_invalid_auth_provider`), PR `#73` (`Allowance.reason` in `{"recurring", "subscription"}` requires `billing_cycle` with valid `interval`/`interval_count` and positive integer `amount_per_cycle` `acp_recurring_allowance_missing_billing_cycle_or_amount_per_cycle`), PR `#57` (`affiliate_attribution` requires `provider` plus `token` or `publisher_id`, is strictly write-only and never echoed in `GET`/list/webhook payloads `acp_affiliate_attribution_leaked_in_read_or_webhook`, and rejects idempotency conflicts or PII `acp_affiliate_attribution_idempotency_conflict_or_pii`), and PRs `#54`/`#45` (write-only `CancelSessionRequest.intent_trace` with `<= 500` char `trace_summary` and flat scalar `metadata` `acp_intent_trace_invalid_or_leaked_in_response`, plus Lodging checkout `quote_token` revalidation `acp_lodging_checkout_quote_token_not_revalidated`).
- **中文**: `verify_acp_recurring_allowance_affiliate_attribution_intent_trace_and_3ds_channel` 强制执行 ACP PR `#85`/`#80`（从 3DS `AuthenticationMetadata` 移除废弃 `channel` 字段 `acp_3ds_authentication_metadata_deprecated_channel_present` 并要求 `PaymentProvider.merchant_id` 必填）、PR `#73`（委托支付 `Allowance.reason` 为 `recurring`/`subscription` 时强制要求 `billing_cycle` 与正整数 `amount_per_cycle` `acp_recurring_allowance_missing_billing_cycle_or_amount_per_cycle`）、PR `#57`（`affiliate_attribution` 联盟归因仅写不读严禁在查询或 Webhook 响应回显 `acp_affiliate_attribution_leaked_in_read_or_webhook`，并拦截同幂等键载荷冲突或 PII）以及 PR `#54`/`#45`（结账取消 `intent_trace` 扁平标量 `metadata` 与仅写保护，以及酒店垂直域 `quote_token` 执行边界重校验）。

### 71.3 Google AP2 `AP2-Haggle` Negotiation (`#229`), Solana Pay `reference` & Algorand `av:` Mandate Binding (`#228`, `#218`), Credential Sanitization (`#212`) & ADK State `.model_dump()` (`#188`)
- **English**: `verify_ap2_haggle_negotiation_solana_algorand_binding_and_credential_sanitization` enforces Google AP2 PR `#229` (`ap2haggle/ap2/extensions/haggle/v0.1`: intermediate `Offer`s remain unsigned, negotiation respects `max_rounds`, `buyer_walk_away_price`, and `merchant_cost_floor`, and an accepted `NegotiationOutcome` MUST carry a merchant-signed `CartMandate` `ap2_haggle_negotiation_constraints_or_signed_outcome_violated`), PRs `#228` & `#218` (human-present `crypto-solana` single-use `reference` pubkey binding and `crypto-algo` USDC ASA transfer note `f"av:{token[:20]}"` binding to `PaymentMandate` `ap2_crypto_solana_or_algorand_mandate_binding_mismatch`), and PRs `#212` & `#188` (opaque `credential_reference` sanitization without raw wallet secret exposure, `payment_mandate_payer == token_owner` verification `ap2_credential_response_unsanitized_or_payer_token_mismatch`, and `.model_dump()` serialization before storing Pydantic mandates in ADK `sqlite_session_service` `ap2_adk_session_state_unserialized_pydantic_model`).
- **中文**: `verify_ap2_haggle_negotiation_solana_algorand_binding_and_credential_sanitization` 强制执行 Google AP2 PR `#229`（`AP2-Haggle` 实时多轮议价：中间 `Offer` 不签名、受最大轮次/买家底价/商户成本底线约束，且成交 `NegotiationOutcome` 必须附带商户签名 `CartMandate` `ap2_haggle_negotiation_constraints_or_signed_outcome_violated`）、PR `#228`/`#218`（Solana Pay 单次 `reference` 公钥绑定与 Algorand USDC ASA `av:{token[:20]}` 备注前缀对 `PaymentMandate` 的链上绑定 `ap2_crypto_solana_or_algorand_mandate_binding_mismatch`）以及 PR `#212`/`#188`（支付凭据响应脱敏为不透明 `credential_reference`、校验 `payment_mandate_payer == token_owner` 并要求 ADK 会话状态在落库前调用 `.model_dump()`）。

### 71.4 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 Banking Read-Log Allowlist (`#329`), Multi-Item Retail Variant Pricing (`#322`), Telecom VPN `deepcopy` Isolation (`#301`) & Replay Hallucination No-Op / Interleaved Thinking (`#273`, `#264`)
- **English**: `verify_tau2_banking_read_allowlist_retail_modify_items_vpn_deepcopy_and_replay_hallucination` enforces Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#329` (`banking_knowledge` `call_discoverable_agent_tool` logs to `agent_discoverable_tools` only when `mutates_state or tool_name in read_log_allowlist` `tau2_banking_discoverable_tool_extra_read_logged_to_db`), PR `#322` (retail `modify_pending_order_items` assigns each item its own replacement variant's price and options `tau2_retail_multi_item_modification_variant_price_cross_contamination`), PR `#301` (`copy.deepcopy(default_vpn_details)` in `_connect_vpn()` preventing cross-simulation `server_performance = "POOR"` state corruption `tau2_telecom_vpn_default_details_shared_reference_corruption`), and PRs `#273` & `#264` (`Environment.set_state()` skipping hallucinated unknown tool calls as a no-op during replay instead of raising `ValueError`, while preserving `raw_data` for interleaved thinking `tau2_replay_unknown_tool_raised_or_interleaved_thinking_stripped`).
- **中文**: `verify_tau2_banking_read_allowlist_retail_modify_items_vpn_deepcopy_and_replay_hallucination` 强制执行 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#329`（`banking_knowledge` 仅在写操作或命中 `read_log_allowlist` 时记录 `agent_discoverable_tools`，防止知识库额外读调用污染 DB 哈希 `tau2_banking_discoverable_tool_extra_read_logged_to_db`）、PR `#322`（零售 `modify_pending_order_items` 多商品改单按各自替换变体独立赋价与选项 `tau2_retail_multi_item_modification_variant_price_cross_contamination`）、PR `#301`（`_connect_vpn()` 使用 `copy.deepcopy(default_vpn_details)` 阻断并发仿真间共享状态污染 `tau2_telecom_vpn_default_details_shared_reference_corruption`）以及 PR `#273`/`#264`（重放时对未知幻觉工具执行空操作跳过而非抛出 `ValueError` 并保留交错思考 `raw_data`）。

## 73. Stage 74 UCP Lodging Booking / Vertical-Agnostic Token Binding / Location Spatial Relations / Constraint Path, ACP Mandatory Idempotency / `/.well-known/acp.json` / `sale` Extension, Google AP2 RFC 8785 JCS Cart Hash / KB-SD-JWT Per-Hop `aud`/`nonce` / Coinbase x402 Stream Cloning & Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 Gemini 8kHz Telephony / Silent `GoAway` Reconnect Guards (`lab/ucp_lodging_token_binding_acp_idempotency_wellknown_ap2_jcs_sdjwt_x402_tau2_gemini_guard.py`)

### 73.1 UCP Lodging Booking & Tri-State Cancellation Policy (`#780`), Vertical-Agnostic Token Binding vs Participant Authorization (`#762`, `#746`), Location Spatial Relations (`#766`, `#765`, `#753`) & Request Constraint Path Targeting (`#757`, `#744`)
- **English**: `verify_ucp_lodging_booking_token_binding_location_spatial_and_constraint_path` enforces UCP PR `#780` (`dev.ucp.lodging.booking` compound `stays[]` with `stay_dates`, platform-supplied two-tier `guests[]` / `guest_assignments[]` roles `primary_guest`/`additional_guest`, and `dev.ucp.lodging.policy.cancellation` tri-state `refundability` in `{"refundable", "partially_refundable", "non_refundable"}` `ucp_lodging_stay_guest_assignment_or_cancellation_policy_invalid`), PRs `#762` & `#746` (vertical-agnostic `common/types/binding.json` `{type, id}` comparing `type` and `id` only while verifying participant `identity` separately at burn `ucp_token_binding_coupled_to_participant_identity_or_invalid_shape`), PRs `#766`/`#765`/`#753` (root-level `distance`/`serves` spatial relations, `filters.items[]` replacing `filters.inventory`, batch Lookup `inputs[]` correlation, and self-describing `amenities` map `ucp_location_spatial_filter_or_amenity_description_violation`), and PRs `#757`/`#744` (outer-only RFC 9535 `path` targeting and non-empty `anyOf` on Request Constraints `ucp_request_constraint_inner_path_or_empty_anyof`).
- **中文**: `verify_ucp_lodging_booking_token_binding_location_spatial_and_constraint_path` 强制执行 UCP PR `#780`（`dev.ucp.lodging.booking` 复合房源绑定 `stays[]`、双层住客池 `guests[]` / `guest_assignments[]` 与三态取消政策 `dev.ucp.lodging.policy.cancellation` `ucp_lodging_stay_guest_assignment_or_cancellation_policy_invalid`）、PR `#762`/`#746`（跨行业解耦的 `common/types/binding.json` `{type, id}` 资源绑定与独立调用方身份 `identity` 烧毁校验 `ucp_token_binding_coupled_to_participant_identity_or_invalid_shape`）、PR `#766`/`#765`/`#753`（顶层空间关系 `distance`/`serves`、`filters.items[]` 替代状态库存过滤、批量 Lookup `inputs[]` 关联与自描述 `amenities` `ucp_location_spatial_filter_or_amenity_description_violation`）以及 PR `#757`/`#744`（仅外层 RFC 9535 `path` 路径寻址与 `anyOf` 约束 `ucp_request_constraint_inner_path_or_empty_anyof`）。

### 73.2 ACP Mandatory POST Idempotency (`#121`), `/.well-known/acp.json` Discovery (`#137`), `sale` Extension & `DiscountsResponse.rejected[]` (`#129`, `#123`), and `Message*`/`Refund.amount`/`IIN`/`risk_signals` Alignment (`#262`, `#249`, `#214`, `#116`)
- **English**: `verify_acp_mandatory_idempotency_wellknown_discovery_sale_and_schema_alignment` enforces ACP PR `#121` (mandatory `Idempotency-Key` on POST with `400 idempotency_key_required`, `409 idempotency_in_flight` + `Retry-After`, `422 idempotency_conflict`, and `Idempotent-Replayed: true`), PR `#137` (public unauthenticated `/.well-known/acp.json` discovery document `acp_well_known_discovery_document_invalid`), PRs `#129` & `#123` (`discounts.rejected[]`, `frictionless.type == "low_risk"`, and `sale` extension referencing session `sales[]` with `compare_at_amount > amount` `acp_discount_rejected_or_sale_extension_invalid`), and PRs `#262`, `#249`, `#214`, `#116` (strict `MessageInfo`/`Warning`/`Error` fields, integer minor-unit `Refund.amount`, empty `risk_signals: []` support, and 6–8 digit `PaymentMethodCard.iin` `acp_message_refund_iin_or_risk_signals_schema_mismatch`).
- **中文**: `verify_acp_mandatory_idempotency_wellknown_discovery_sale_and_schema_alignment` 强制执行 ACP PR `#121`（POST 请求强制携带 `Idempotency-Key`，支持 `400 idempotency_key_required`、`409 idempotency_in_flight` + `Retry-After`、`422 idempotency_conflict` 与 `Idempotent-Replayed: true` 响应头）、PR `#137`（免认证公开 `/.well-known/acp.json` 发现文档 `acp_well_known_discovery_document_invalid`）、PR `#129`/`#123`（`discounts.rejected[]`、3DS `frictionless.type == "low_risk"` 及 `sale` 促销价格上下文扩展 `acp_discount_rejected_or_sale_extension_invalid`）以及 PR `#262`/`#249`/`#214`/`#116`（剔除 `Message*` 幻觉字段、`Refund.amount` 强制整数最小货币单位、允许空 `risk_signals: []` 与 6–8 位 `iin` `acp_message_refund_iin_or_risk_signals_schema_mismatch`）。

### 73.3 Google AP2 RFC 8785 JCS Cart-to-Payment Hash (`#253`), KB-SD-JWT Per-Hop & Terminal `aud`/`nonce` (`#342`, `#326`, `#313`), Immediate Execution Window (`#318`), Budget Major-vs-Minor Units (`#340`) & Coinbase x402 Stream Cloning / Multi-Rail `accepts` / Signer `.address` (`#323`, `#306`, `#297`, `#277`)
- **English**: `verify_ap2_jcs_cart_hash_sdjwt_hops_execution_window_and_x402_stream_accepts` enforces Google AP2 PR `#253` (`cart_mandate_hash = hex(sha256(JCS(CartMandate)))` with `null` fields stripped `ap2_jcs_cart_mandate_hash_mismatch`), PRs `#342`/`#326`/`#313` (KB-SD-JWT per-hop and terminal `aud`/`nonce` presence and expected-claim verification `ap2_kb_sdjwt_hop_or_terminal_aud_nonce_unbound`), PRs `#318` & `#340` (omitted `execution_date` evaluated at current UTC clock against `[not_before, not_after]` and `payment.budget.max` major-unit $\times 100$ normalization `ap2_immediate_payment_outside_execution_window` / `ap2_budget_major_vs_minor_unit_overflow`), and Coinbase x402 PRs `#323`/`#306`/`#297`/`#277` (cloning `ReadableStream` bodies before 402 probes, skipping unknown CAIP-2 `accepts[]` entries to select payable rails, requiring top-level 20-byte hex signer `.address`, and complete EIP-3009 EIP-712 domain `x402_stream_body_accepts_or_signer_address_violation`).
- **中文**: `verify_ap2_jcs_cart_hash_sdjwt_hops_execution_window_and_x402_stream_accepts` 强制执行 Google AP2 PR `#253`（剥离 `null` 后的 RFC 8785 JCS `cart_mandate_hash` 绑定 `ap2_jcs_cart_mandate_hash_mismatch`）、PR `#342`/`#326`/`#313`（KB-SD-JWT 中间跳与终端跳 `aud`/`nonce` 逐跳及期望值强校验 `ap2_kb_sdjwt_hop_or_terminal_aud_nonce_unbound`）、PR `#318`/`#340`（缺省 `execution_date` 按当前 UTC 时间校验 `[not_before, not_after]` 窗口及 `payment.budget.max` 主单位与次单位换算统一）以及 Coinbase x402 PR `#323`/`#306`/`#297`/`#277`（流式请求体克隆重试、忽略未知 CAIP-2 `accepts[]` 轨道择优支付、顶层签名器 `.address` 强校验与 EIP-3009 EIP-712 域完整性 `x402_stream_body_accepts_or_signer_address_violation`）。

### 73.4 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 Gemini 8kHz Native Telephony & SlidingWindow (`#544`, `#511`, `#510`, `#509`), Silent `GoAway` Reconnect Deadline (`#461`), Full-Duplex Nonblocking User Generation (`#525`, `#522`) & Empty Turn Retry (`#471`)
- **English**: `verify_tau2_gemini_8khz_telephony_goaway_reconnect_nonblocking_duplex_and_empty_turn_retry` enforces Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PRs `#544`/`#511`/`#510`/`#509` (8kHz native telephony input without client-side `ratecv` upsampling, `START_SENSITIVITY_HIGH`, `SlidingWindow` context compression, and `en-US` transcription hint `tau2_gemini_telephony_8khz_or_sliding_window_misconfigured`), PR `#461` (active `_reconnect_deadline` checking when the Gemini Live server goes silent after `GoAway` `tau2_gemini_goaway_silent_server_missed_reconnect`), PRs `#525` & `#522` (nonblocking background user generation and text-only LLM history in full-duplex streaming `tau2_full_duplex_tick_blocked_or_duplicate_user_init`), and PR `#471` (single retry with `###STOP###` reminder on empty text-mode user-simulator completions `tau2_empty_user_simulator_completion_unretried_crash`).
- **中文**: `verify_tau2_gemini_8khz_telephony_goaway_reconnect_nonblocking_duplex_and_empty_turn_retry` 强制执行 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#544`/`#511`/`#510`/`#509`（Gemini Live 原生 8kHz 电话采样率直传、`START_SENSITIVITY_HIGH` 高灵敏度语音端点检测、`SlidingWindow` 上下文压缩与默认 `en-US` 转写语言提示 `tau2_gemini_telephony_8khz_or_sliding_window_misconfigured`）、PR `#461`（服务端发送 `GoAway` 后静默时主动触发 `_reconnect_deadline` 重连 `tau2_gemini_goaway_silent_server_missed_reconnect`）、PR `#525`/`#522`（全双工非阻塞后台用户语音生成与纯文本历史去重初始化 `tau2_full_duplex_tick_blocked_or_duplicate_user_init`）以及 PR `#471`（文本模式用户模拟器空回复自动附带 `###STOP###` 提示重试 `tau2_empty_user_simulator_completion_unretried_crash`）。

## 75. Stage 76 Guardrails: UCP Payment Terms Selection & Deterministic Location Hours / Core Policies / Media Variants (`#741`, `#736`, `#712`, `#692`, `#690`, `#689`, `#688`, `#687`, `#671`, `#603`, `#589`, `#572`), ACP Native Order Lifecycle & Message Resolution / Version Negotiation (`#245`, `#143`, `#118`, `#117`, `#115`, `#106`, `#101`, `#100`, `#99`), Google AP2 Multi-Hop `~~` SD-JWT / Payment Evidence Frame (PEF) & Coinbase x402 Bazaar / Facilitator / Attestation (`#371`, `#350`, `#276`, `#274`, `#272`, `#271`, `#269`, `#159` & x402 `#317`, `#312`, `#283`, `#244`, `#225`, `#206`, `#180`, `#136`, `#135`), and Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 Voice Role-Flip Whitespace / Qwen Nested Tools / Usage-Cost & Parallel WorkQueue Leases (`#546`, `#538`, `#535`, `#526`, `#523`, `#521`, `#483`, `#456`, `#455`, `#454`, `#450`, `#449`, `#440`)

### 75.1 UCP Payment Terms Checkout Selection (`#712`, `#692`, `#603`, `#741`), Location Deterministic Operating Hours (`#687`, `#589`), Core `policies[]` Disclosure Linkage (`#572`), Method-Scoped Destination Authorship (`#689`, `#688`, `#671`) & Media Variants (`#690`)
- **English**: `verify_ucp_payment_terms_deterministic_hours_policies_and_media_variants` in `lab/ucp_payment_terms_hours_policies_acp_order_lifecycle_ap2_pef_x402_tau2_voice_guard.py` enforces UCP PRs `#712`/`#692`/`#603`/`#741`/`#736` (optional checkout-selected `payment.selected_term_id`, scalar `schedules[].amount` replacing single-element `totals[]`, prohibition of deprecated `instruments[].term_refs`, prohibition of subscription recurrence in payment terms `UCP_SUBSCRIPTION_IN_PAYMENT_TERMS_FORBIDDEN`, prohibition of composing multi-schedule payment terms with `split_payments` `UCP_MULTI_SCHEDULE_TERM_SPLIT_PAYMENT_COMPOSITION_FORBIDDEN`, and mandatory `payment_term_changed` warning when the Business alters or invalidates a selected term), PR `#687`/`#589` (Location deterministic operating hours with Business IANA `timezone`, inseparable `opens`/`closes` rejecting `00:00`–`00:00`, inclusive `valid_from`/`valid_through` exceptions rejecting intersecting non-identical ranges, and explicit RFC 3339 `open_at` filter rejecting server-clock `open_now`), and PRs `#572`/`#688`/`#671`/`#690` (core `policies[]` `final_sale` XOR `window` with matching `presentation: "disclosure"` warning at `code == policy.type`, Business-only `pickup` destination authorship + provisional-only `context.location`, and `external_video` vs playable `video` `sources[]` with `mime_type`).
- **中文**: `verify_ucp_payment_terms_deterministic_hours_policies_and_media_variants` 强制执行 UCP PR `#712`/`#692`/`#603`/`#741`/`#736`（可选结账账期协商 `payment.selected_term_id`、标量 `schedules[].amount` 替代单元素 `totals[]`、禁用废弃 `instruments[].term_refs`、禁止将订阅周期编码进付款账期、禁止多期账期与 `split_payments` 拆单组合 `UCP_MULTI_SCHEDULE_TERM_SPLIT_PAYMENT_COMPOSITION_FORBIDDEN`、以及商家变更账期时强制发出 `payment_term_changed` 告警）、PR `#687`/`#589`（Location 确定性营业时间模型：强制商家 IANA `timezone`、成对 `opens`/`closes` 并拒绝 `00:00`–`00:00`、闭区间 `valid_from`/`valid_through` 异常日期禁止非全等重叠、以显式带偏移量 `open_at` 替代隐式服务器时钟 `open_now`）以及 PR `#572`/`#688`/`#671`/`#690`（核心 `policies[]` 强制 `window` 与 `final_sale` 互斥并联动 `presentation: "disclosure"` 告警、`pickup` 自提目的地仅允许商家响应生成且 `context.location` 禁止自动选中目的地、以及 `external_video` 与 `video` 多源 `mime_type` 校验）。

### 75.2 ACP Native Order Post-Purchase Lifecycle (`#106`), `Message.resolution` & `intervention_required` vs `requires_3ds` (`#100`, `#143`, `#118`, `#115`), and `supported_versions[]` Negotiation / Session Total Parity (`#99`, `#117`, `#245`, `#101`)
- **English**: `verify_acp_post_purchase_order_lifecycle_resolution_intervention_and_version_negotiation` enforces ACP PR `#106` (SEP #105 Native Orders post-purchase lifecycle: `0 <= quantity.shipped <= quantity.ordered`, `fulfillments[]` `type` in `{"shipping", "pickup", "digital"}` with cumulative fulfilled item quantities bounded by `ordered`, and unified `adjustments[]` in `{"refund", "credit", "return", "dispute", "chargeback"}` with integer minor-unit `amount`), PRs `#100`/`#143`/`#118`/`#115` (`Message.resolution` closed enum `{"recoverable", "requires_buyer_input", "requires_buyer_review"}`, `intervention_required` for capability mismatch vs `requires_3ds` for missing `authentication_result` on `complete`, and strict separation between top-level HTTP `Error` and in-session `MessageError`), and PRs `#99`/`#117` (non-empty `supported_versions[]` on `unsupported_api_version`/`missing_api_version` errors and continuous session fulfillment cost/total parity across `update` -> `complete`/`cancel`).
- **中文**: `verify_acp_post_purchase_order_lifecycle_resolution_intervention_and_version_negotiation` 强制执行 ACP PR `#106`（SEP #105 原生订单售后生命周期：`0 <= quantity.shipped <= quantity.ordered`、`fulfillments[]` 三态枚举与累计发货量上限守恒、以及 `adjustments[]` 五类逆向调整整数次单位金额）、PR `#100`/`#143`/`#118`/`#115`（`Message.resolution` 闭集枚举、能力不匹配 `intervention_required` 与完成态缺失认证结果 `requires_3ds` 的严格区分、顶层 HTTP `Error` 与会话内 `MessageError` 边界隔离）以及 PR `#99`/`#117`（版本不匹配错误必填 `supported_versions[]` 数组与连续会话 `update` -> `complete`/`cancel` 履约费用及总价一致性）。

### 75.3 Google AP2 Multi-Hop `~~` SD-JWT Delegation Chain (`#350`, `#371`, `#159`), Payment Evidence Frame (`PEF`) / Compliance / Settlement / Trust Envelopes & Session Spend Cap (`#274`, `#272`, `#271`, `#269`, `#276`) & Coinbase x402 Bazaar Format / Public Facilitator / Multi-Chain USDC / Counterparty Attestation (`#317`, `#312`, `#283`, `#244`, `#225`, `#206`, `#180`, `#136`, `#135`)
- **English**: `verify_ap2_sdjwt_delegation_chain_pef_attestation_and_x402_bazaar_facilitator_guard` enforces Google AP2 PRs `#350`/`#371`/`#159` (splitting SD-JWT delegation chains on `~~` before single `~` so hop-1+ disclosures are not dropped as a trailing KB-JWT, and serializing Pydantic models via `.model_dump()` before storing in `tool_context.state`), AP2 PRs `#274`/`#272`/`#271`/`#269`/`#276` (Payment Evidence Frame `PEF` closed 5-value `claim_type`, RFC 8785 JCS SHA-256 `frame_id` and `receipt_hash`, Compliance Receipt `ALLOW`/`REFER`/`DENY`, Settlement Attestation `SETTLED`/`PENDING_FINALITY`/`REVERSED`, Composite Trust Verdict, and `402 agent_spend_cap_exceeded` session spend cap), and Coinbase x402 PRs `#283`/`#206`/`#244`/`#180`/`#225`/`#136` (`ajv-formats` URI/date-time validation on Bazaar schemas, blocking testnet-only `https://x402.org/facilitator` on Base Mainnet `eip155:8453`, Celo/Arbitrum EIP-3009 6-decimal USDC registry verification, and independent third-party DID Ed25519 counterparty fact/trust attestation without subjective judgment fields).
- **中文**: `verify_ap2_sdjwt_delegation_chain_pef_attestation_and_x402_bazaar_facilitator_guard` 强制执行 Google AP2 PR `#350`/`#371`/`#159`（SD-JWT 多跳委托链先按 `~~` 再按单 `~` 切分以防次跳披露字段被误判为尾部 KB-JWT 丢失，以及会话状态写入前强制 `.model_dump()` 序列化）、AP2 PR `#274`/`#272`/`#271`/`#269`/`#276`（支付证据帧 `PEF` 五态 `claim_type` 与 JCS SHA-256 `frame_id`/`receipt_hash` 推导、合规收据 `ALLOW`/`REFER`/`DENY`、结算证明 `SETTLED`/`PENDING_FINALITY`/`REVERSED`、复合信任裁决与会话支出上限 `402 agent_spend_cap_exceeded`）以及 Coinbase x402 PR `#283`/`#206`/`#244`/`#180`/`#225`/`#136`（Bazaar `ajv-formats` 格式校验、拦截测试网公共 Facilitator 误配 Base 主网 `eip155:8453` 导致 `500` 崩溃、Celo/Arbitrum EIP-3009 USDC 注册表核验、以及独立第三方 DID Ed25519 事实证明且禁含主观裁决字段）。

### 75.4 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 Voice Role-Flip Whitespace Guard (`#440`), Tri-State Realtime User Generation (`#526`, `#523`, `#521`), Qwen `qwen3.5-omni-plus-realtime` Nested Tools (`#449`, `#450`), Audio-Native Usage/Cost & Provider Concurrency Caps (`#454`, `#456`), Parallel `WorkQueue` Leases (`#455`) & $\tau$-Voice Submission Boundary (`#483`, `#546`, `#538`, `#535`)
- **English**: `verify_tau2_voice_role_flip_qwen_nested_tools_usage_cost_and_parallel_lease_guard` enforces Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#440` (dropping whitespace-only turns in both `user->assistant` and `agent->user` role flips unless carrying `tool_calls`), PRs `#526`/`#523`/`#521` (tri-state `realtime_generation` defaulting to enabled for OpenAI Live V3 and disabled for other providers unless overridden), PRs `#449`/`#450` (`qwen3.5-omni-plus-realtime` requirement for tool calling, nested `{"type": "function", "function": {...}}` schema, stripping `tool_choice` and VAD `prefix_padding_ms`, and `Tina` default voice), and PRs `#454`/`#456`/`#455`/`#483`/`#546`/`#535` (`UsageRecord` `delta` vs `cumulative` last-value-wins per scope, `cost = None` instead of silent `$0` on unknown/unverified models, default provider concurrency caps `40/10/5`, single-writer controller checkpointing with capped lease retries, and standard $\tau$-voice track boundary + sanitized reviewer exports).
- **中文**: `verify_tau2_voice_role_flip_qwen_nested_tools_usage_cost_and_parallel_lease_guard` 强制执行 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#440`（语音模拟器双向角色翻转时丢弃无工具调用的纯空白轮次）、PR `#526`/`#523`/`#521`（三态 `realtime_generation` 默认仅对 OpenAI Live V3 开启并支持 CLI 显式覆盖）、PR `#449`/`#450`（Qwen 实时工具调用强制升级 `qwen3.5-omni-plus-realtime`、采用嵌套 `function` 工具格式、剔除不支持的 `tool_choice` 与 VAD `prefix_padding_ms` 并默认使用 `Tina` 音色）以及 PR `#454`/`#456`/`#455`/`#483`/`#546`/`#535`（`UsageRecord` 增量与累计最后值胜出计费、未知模型返回 `cost = None` 严禁静默记 `$0`、语音提供商默认并发上限 `40/10/5`、并行 `WorkQueue` 控制器单写检查点与租约重试上限、以及 $\tau$-voice 标准赛道边界与人工评审隐私脱敏）。

- **中文**: `lab/ucp_payment_terms_hours_policies_acp_order_lifecycle_ap2_pef_x402_tau2_voice_bench.py` 构建覆盖 Stage 76 四大护栏域的 16 个确定性评测场景（`PT-01..PT-16`）：AI-Native 护栏实现 **16/16 (100.0%)** 准确率与 **100.0%** 逆序扰动稳定性（对比经典三层架构基线 **4/16, 25.0%**），中位延迟 **0.35 ms vs. 4.68 ms（13.4x 加速）**，并生成中英双语自包含 7-Card 4:5 视觉评测报告 [`index.html`（中文）](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-payment-terms-hours-policies-acp-order-lifecycle-ap2-pef-x402-tau2-voice-bench/index.html) 与 [`index.en.html`（English）](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-payment-terms-hours-policies-acp-order-lifecycle-ap2-pef-x402-tau2-voice-bench/index.en.html)。

## 77. Stage 78 Guardrails: UCP Sale-Basis Quantity Steps / Non-Empty Actions & Policies / Loyalty Breakdown / Event Feed & Post-Strip Array Elision (`#686`, `#679`, `#673`, `#669`, `#662`, `#661`, `#653`, `#651`, `#646`, `#642`), ACP Delegated 3DS Authentication API & Payment Handlers / Discount Extension (`#93`, `#90`, `#89`, `#85`, `#80`, `#77`, `#76`, `#66`), Google AP2 Cross-Merchant Atomic Budget Authority & TrustBoost Pre-LLM PII Sanitization (`#267`, `#252`, `#243`, `#233`), and Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 `gptlive` Full-Duplex Provider / Transcription Retry / Viewer Truncation / Active Reward Gates & Banking Answer-Key Scrubbing (`#434`, `#429`, `#426`, `#418`, `#414`, `#412`, `#411`, `#409`, `#408`)

### 77.1 UCP Sale-Basis Quantity Steps (`#653`), Non-Empty `actions` Map (`#669`), Non-Empty `policies[].applies_to` (`#661`), Loyalty `earning_forecast.breakdown` Sum (`#662`), Request-Shape Omission (`#651`, `#646`) & `event_feed` / Post-Strip Array Elision (`#686`, `#679`, `#673`)
- **English**: `verify_ucp_sale_basis_steps_loyalty_actions_policies_and_elision_guard` in `lab/ucp_sale_basis_loyalty_actions_acp_delegated_auth_handlers_ap2_budget_pii_tau2_gptlive_guard.py` enforces UCP PR `#653` (integer step `quantity > 0` with `quantity_unit` `(unit, scale, display_text)`, prohibiting non-zero `scale` on `C62` (`each`), verifying $\text{round\_half\_up}(\text{price} \times \text{quantity} \times 10^{-\text{scale}})$ against authoritative line subtotal, and validating positive integer `unit_price` comparators with matching unit/currency), PRs `#669`/`#661`/`#662` (`minProperties: 1` on `actions`, `minItems: 1` on `policies[].applies_to` using `@.tags`/`@.categories` rather than nonexistent `@.category`, and minor-unit `loyalty.earning_forecast.breakdown` sum invariant), PRs `#651`/`#646` (rejecting server-assigned `ucp_request: "omit"` fields `status`/`totals`/`continue_url`/`expires_at`/`id` on checkout/cart request bodies), and PRs `#673`/`#686`/`#679` (`event_feed` HTTPS + `{"webhook", "sse", "poll"}` transports, post-strip array elision indexing, and MCP `complete_checkout`/`cancel_checkout` idempotency conflict JSON-RPC `-32000`).
- **中文**: `verify_ucp_sale_basis_steps_loyalty_actions_policies_and_elision_guard` 强制执行 UCP PR `#653`（正整数步长 `quantity` 与 `quantity_unit` `(unit, scale, display_text)`，禁止 `C62`（件）使用非零 `scale`，校验 $\text{round\_half\_up}(\text{price} \times \text{quantity} \times 10^{-\text{scale}})$ 行小计舍入守恒与正整数 `unit_price` 比较基准）、PR `#669`/`#661`/`#662`（`actions` 映射非空 `minProperties: 1`、`policies[].applies_to` 非空且禁用不存在的标量 `@.category` 路径、以及积分 `loyalty.earning_forecast.breakdown` 次单位金额求和守恒）、PR `#651`/`#646`（请求体严禁携带服务端只读 `ucp_request: "omit"` 字段及废弃 `id`）以及 PR `#673`/`#686`/`#679`（`event_feed` 事件流契约、数组省略符剔除后索引记录与 MCP 幂等冲突 `-32000` 映射）。

### 77.2 ACP Delegated 3DS Authentication API (`#93`, `#80`, `#85`, `#66`) & Payment Handlers / Discount Extension Framework (`#90`, `#89`, `#77`, `#76`)
- **English**: `verify_acp_delegated_authentication_and_payment_handlers_guard` enforces ACP PRs `#93`/`#80`/`#85`/`#66` (Delegated Authentication API requiring `create`, `authenticate`, and `retrieve` endpoints, mandatory `authentication_session_id`, 3DS Method fingerprint execution before `authenticate`, CReq challenge completion before `retrieve`, removal of deprecated seller `AuthenticationMetadata.channel` while requiring `channel` in `create` or `authenticate`, and `409 Conflict` on idempotency mismatch) and PRs `#90`/`#89` (replacing legacy string `payment_methods[]` with reverse-DNS `capabilities.payment.handlers[]`, binding `config.merchant_id` to `delegate_payment` `constraints.merchant_id`, and verifying Discount Extension allocation sums and rejection `reason_code`s).
- **中文**: `verify_acp_delegated_authentication_and_payment_handlers_guard` 强制执行 ACP PR `#93`/`#80`/`#85`/`#66`（委托 3DS 认证 API 必选三端点 `create`/`authenticate`/`retrieve`、强制 `authentication_session_id`、按序执行设备指纹与 CReq 挑战后再调用 `retrieve`、移除 `AuthenticationMetadata.channel` 并在委托认证请求中提供 `channel`、幂等冲突返回 `409`）以及 PR `#90`/`#89`（以反向域名 `capabilities.payment.handlers[]` 替代旧版字符串 `payment_methods[]`、校验 `config.merchant_id` 与 `delegate_payment` 约束一致，并核验折扣扩展分摊求和与拒单 `reason_code`）。

### 77.3 Google AP2 Cross-Merchant Atomic Budget Authority (`#252`), TrustBoost Pre-LLM PII Sanitization + x402 Solana Proof (`#267`) & Normative Verifier `checkout_hash` Obligation (`#243`, `#233`)
- **English**: `verify_ap2_cross_merchant_budget_authority_and_trustboost_pii_guard` enforces Google AP2 PR `#252` (blocking isolated per-merchant `MandateContext` evaluation that enables cross-merchant double-spend race conditions, and requiring an atomic 4-verb `{"authorize", "commit", "refund", "query"}` Budget Authority where `committed + active_holds + requested <= total_budget`), PR `#267` (sanitizing PII before LLM context ingestion via x402-paid TrustBoost and verifying the Solana mainnet anchor proof), and PRs `#243`/`#233` (normative verifier obligation to recompute and match `checkout_hash`).
- **中文**: `verify_ap2_cross_merchant_budget_authority_and_trustboost_pii_guard` 强制执行 Google AP2 PR `#252`（阻断跨商户并发结账因独立 `MandateContext` 导致的预算超支竞态，强制使用支持 `authorize`/`commit`/`refund`/`query` 四原语的外部原子预算中心并校验 `committed + active_holds + requested <= total_budget`）、PR `#267`（在载荷进入 LLM 上下文前通过 x402 结算完成 TrustBoost PII 脱敏并核验 Solana 链上锚定证明）以及 PR `#243`/`#233`（验证方重算并比对 `checkout_hash` 的规范性义务）。

### 77.4 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 `gptlive` Full-Duplex Provider (`#409`), Transient Transcription Retry (`#418`), Viewer Truncation (`#434`), Active Reward Gates (`#411`) & Banking Answer-Key Leak Scrubbing (`#429`, `#414`, `#412`, `#408`)
- **English**: `verify_tau2_gptlive_provider_transcription_retry_and_banking_leak_guard` enforces Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#409` (`gptlive` audio-native provider using `delegation.responses.tools` without `response.create`, joining `call_id`/`name` from `response.output_item.added` via `item_id`, RMS hysteresis silence gating, and `GPTLIVE_TEST_ENABLED=1` alpha gating), PRs `#418`/`#434`/`#411` (retrying transient transcription errors instead of returning empty transcripts, truncating `tau2 view` tool results to `500` chars by default, and requiring non-empty criteria for active `COMMUNICATE`/`NL_ASSERTION` reward gates), and PRs `#429`/`#414`/`#412`/`#408` (coercing numeric string tool args, scrubbing embedded answer-key annotations like `SHOULD BE FREE` / `AFTER FREE ALLOWANCE` from `db.json`, and enforcing the policy-faithful `$14.50` Light Blue ATM fee refund on T074).
- **中文**: `verify_tau2_gptlive_provider_transcription_retry_and_banking_leak_guard` 强制执行 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#409`（`gptlive` 全双工语音适配器采用 `delegation.responses.tools` 且禁发 `response.create`、通过 `item_id` 关联恢复 `call_id`/`name`、启用 RMS 迟滞静音门限与 `GPTLIVE_TEST_ENABLED=1` 门禁）、PR `#418`/`#434`/`#411`（瞬态转写故障自动重试而非静默返回空文本、`tau2 view` 默认 500 字符截断、活跃奖励门禁非空准则校验）以及 PR `#429`/`#414`/`#412`/`#408`（数值型字符串工具参数一致性兼容、清洗 `db.json` 交易描述中泄漏答案的 `SHOULD BE FREE` / `AFTER FREE ALLOWANCE` 标注，并修正 T074 Light Blue 账户 ATM 费用退款黄金标准为 `$14.50`）。

## 79. Stage 80 Guardrails: UCP Namespace Authority Binding / Per-Segment Buyer Consent / Eligibility Verification Contract / Admission-Time Mandate TTL & Raw `r||s` Signatures (`#590`, `#540`, `#530`, `#507`, `#494`, `#485`, `#458`, `#451`, `#265`, `#250`, `#247`, `#246`), ACP Consumer 3DS / Mixed-Cart Fulfillment / TLS 1.3 / 8-Digit IIN & B2B Decimal Quantities (`#53`, `#52`, `#51`, `#50`, `#49`, `#48`, `#32`, `#9`, `#7`, `#3`, `#1`), Google AP2 RFC 8785 JCS Mandate Binding / DID+STARK Settlement & Visa TAP RFC 9421 Merchant Verifier (`#370`, `#257`, `#251`, `#48`, `#29`, `#20`, TAP `#21`), and Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 Full-Duplex Voice Rescoring / Banking Ordering / Discoverable Reads & LLM Execution Fixes (`#403`, `#402`, `#401`, `#399`, `#397`, `#393`, `#388`, `#362`, `#361`, `#360`, `#358`)

### 79.1 UCP Namespace Authority Binding (`#530`), Per-Segment Buyer Consent (`#451`), Eligibility Verification Contract (`#250`), Admission-Time AP2 Mandate TTL (`#540`) & Raw `r||s` ECDSA Signatures / Embedded `params.error` / Link & Cart Continuity (`#590`, `#494`, `#247`, `#246`)
- **English**: `verify_ucp_authority_consent_eligibility_mandate_ttl_and_signature_guard` in `lab/ucp_authority_consent_eligibility_acp_3ds_fulfillment_ap2_jcs_tap_rfc9421_tau2_duplex_guard.py` enforces UCP PR `#530` (binding a capability's reverse-DNS prefix to the HTTPS origin host of its declared `schema` URL and validating broadened RDNS grammar), PR `#451` (per-purpose and per-segment `buyer.consent` objects with `granted` boolean and `source in {"business", "platform"}`), PR `#250` (requiring `context.eligibility` claims that influenced checkout to resolve as `verified` or `rescinded` before `complete_checkout` and forbidding checkout mutation on verification failure), PR `#540` (evaluating AP2 mandate TTL once at `complete_checkout` admission so post-admission expiry during `complete_in_progress` never aborts settlement), and PRs `#590`/`#494`/`#247`/`#246` (forbidding ASN.1 DER `MEUC`/`MEQC` ECDSA signatures in favor of raw `r||s`, wrapping embedded errors under `params.error`, emitting `ec.link.open_request` when `ec_delegate=link.open`, and preserving cart discount codes into checkout).
- **中文**: `verify_ucp_authority_consent_eligibility_mandate_ttl_and_signature_guard` 强制执行 UCP PR `#530`（反向域名能力前缀与 `schema` HTTPS 域名权威绑定及扩展 RDNS 语法校验）、PR `#451`（按用途与细分维度 `segments` 组织的 `buyer.consent` 同意对象及 `source` 校验）、PR `#250`（影响结账的 `context.eligibility` 权益声明在 `complete_checkout` 时必须完成验证或撤销，且验证失败严禁篡改结账状态）、PR `#540`（AP2 授权 TTL 仅在 `complete_checkout` 准入时评估一次，进入 `complete_in_progress` 后过期不得中断结算）以及 PR `#590`/`#494`/`#247`/`#246`（强制 ECDSA 原始 `r||s` 签名编码并拦截 DER `MEUC`/`MEQC` 前缀、`ec.error` 包裹于 `params.error`、`ec_delegate=link.open` 触发链接打开请求及购物车至结账优惠码连续性）。

### 79.2 ACP Consumer 3DS (`#53`), Mixed-Cart Fulfillment & Contact (`#52`, `#51`), Return Policy Link (`#49`), TLS 1.3 (`#32`), 6–8 Digit Card IIN (`#9`), Exact 4-Digit `display_last4` (`#1`) & B2B Decimal Quantities (`#3`)
- **English**: `verify_acp_consumer_3ds_mixed_fulfillment_iin_and_b2b_quantity_guard` enforces ACP PR `#32` (`TLSv1.3` transport), PRs `#52`/`#51` (shipping fulfillment contact `name` + `phone`/`email`, integer minor-unit `subtotal`/`tax`/`total` on `FulfillmentOptionShipping`, and complete line-item coverage across mixed physical+digital carts), PR `#49` (forbidding deprecated `seller_shop_policies` link type in favor of `return_policy`), and PRs `#9`/`#1`/`#3` (6–8 digit ISO/IEC 7812-1 card `iin`, exact 4-digit `display_last4`, and positive decimal `quantity > 0` for B2B items).
- **中文**: `verify_acp_consumer_3ds_mixed_fulfillment_iin_and_b2b_quantity_guard` 强制执行 ACP PR `#32`（`TLSv1.3` 传输安全）、PR `#52`/`#51`（收货地址必填联系人姓名与电话/邮箱、配送选项 `subtotal`/`tax`/`total` 必须为整数次单位金额、混合购物车实物+数字商品履约全覆盖）、PR `#49`（禁用废弃 `seller_shop_policies` 链接类型并改用 `return_policy`）以及 PR `#9`/`#1`/`#3`（支持 ISO/IEC 7812-1 6–8 位卡 `iin`、严格 4 位 `display_last4` 及 B2B 正小数数量 `quantity > 0`）。

### 79.3 Google AP2 RFC 8785 JCS Cart-to-Payment Mandate Binding (`#251`, `#370`), DID + STARK Settlement (`#257`), Typed `PaymentDetailsModifier` (`#48`), VDC Terminology (`#20`) & Visa TAP RFC 9421 Merchant-Backend Signature Verifier (`#21`)
- **English**: `verify_ap2_jcs_binding_did_stark_and_tap_rfc9421_verifier_guard` enforces Google AP2 PRs `#251`/`#370` (cryptographic `CartMandate` to `PaymentMandate` binding via RFC 8785 JSON Canonicalization Scheme SHA-256 digest), PRs `#257`/`#48`/`#20` (verifying DID delegation + STARK settlement proof when DID settlement is enabled, forbidding untyped `Any` in `PaymentDetailsModifier` for Vertex AI compatibility, and enforcing `verifiable_digital_credential` terminology), and Visa TAP PR `#21` (reconstructing the RFC 9421 signature base ending with `"@signature-params"`, parsing individually quoted covered components `("@authority" "@path")` rather than a single space-joined blob, matching `alg` (`ed25519` / `rsa-pss-sha256`) to the agent registry public key type, and verifying the signature before recording `nonce` in the replay cache).
- **中文**: `verify_ap2_jcs_binding_did_stark_and_tap_rfc9421_verifier_guard` 强制执行 Google AP2 PR `#251`/`#370`（基于 RFC 8785 JCS 规范化 SHA-256 摘要实现 `CartMandate` 与 `PaymentMandate` 跨语言防替换加密绑定）、PR `#257`/`#48`/`#20`（DID 委托链与 STARK 结算承诺核验、禁用 `PaymentDetailsModifier` 未类型化 `Any` 以兼容 Vertex AI、统一 `verifiable_digital_credential` 术语）以及 Visa TAP PR `#21`（商户后端签名验证器必须以 `"@signature-params"` 结尾重构 RFC 9421 签名基串、逐项引号解析覆盖组件而非单字符串拼接、严格匹配 `alg` 与公钥类型防止算法混淆，并在验签通过后才写入 `nonce` 防重放缓存）。

### 79.4 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 Full-Duplex Voice Rescoring (`#399`), Banking Reverse-Chronological Ordering (`#403`), Lost/Stolen Discoverable Read Calls (`#402`), Nested JSON/Numeric Normalization (`#401`, `#397`), Batch Replay Isolation (`#393`), Platinum 10% Cash-Back Doc (`#388`) & Airline/LLM Execution Fixes (`#362`, `#361`, `#360`, `#358`)
- **English**: `verify_tau2_voice_rescoring_banking_ordering_and_llm_execution_guard` enforces Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#399` (passing `FULL_DUPLEX` mode when rescoring voice trajectories so `simulation.ticks` is evaluated instead of `simulation.messages=None`), PRs `#403`/`#402`/`#388` (reverse-chronological transaction ordering with earliest-duplicate dispute targeting on T083/T084, calling unlocked discoverable read tools on lost/stolen debit card tasks T077–T086, and aligning `doc_credit_cards_platinum_rewards_card_002` title to `10%`), PRs `#401`/`#397`/`#393` (normalizing nested JSON strings and int/float numeric args in graders, and isolating single-simulation replay errors during batch runs), and PRs `#362`/`#361`/`#360`/`#358` (restoring cancelled airline seats to inventory, preserving `UserMessage.tool_calls` in LiteLLM conversion, classifying `ContextWindowExceededError` without retrying as `INFRASTRUCTURE_ERROR`, and stripping `temperature=0.0` for reasoning models).
- **中文**: `verify_tau2_voice_rescoring_banking_ordering_and_llm_execution_guard` 强制执行 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#399`（全双工语音轨迹重评分显式传入 `FULL_DUPLEX` 并评估 `simulation.ticks`）、PR `#403`/`#402`/`#388`（银行交易按时间倒序返回且重复交易争议锁定最早一笔、挂失/被盗借记卡黄金轨迹必须实际调用已解锁的可发现读取工具、修正白金卡文档标题为 `10%` 返现）、PR `#401`/`#397`/`#393`（动作评分器归一化嵌套 JSON 字符串与整型/浮点型数值参数、单条回放异常隔离不中断整批评测）以及 PR `#362`/`#361`/`#360`/`#358`（航司退票释放座位库存、LiteLLM 转换保留 `UserMessage.tool_calls`、上下文超限不再误判为基础设施错误重试、推理模型自动剥离 `temperature=0.0`）。

## 80. Stage 81 16-Scenario (`AC-01..AC-16`) Bilingual 7-Card 4:5 Benchmark Suite (`lab/ucp_authority_consent_eligibility_acp_3ds_fulfillment_ap2_jcs_tap_rfc9421_tau2_duplex_bench.py`)

- **English**: `lab/ucp_authority_consent_eligibility_acp_3ds_fulfillment_ap2_jcs_tap_rfc9421_tau2_duplex_bench.py` evaluates 16 deterministic scenarios (`AC-01..AC-16`) spanning all four Stage 80 guardrail domains: AI-Native guardrails achieve **16/16 (100.0%)** accuracy and **100.0%** option-reversal consistency (vs. **4/16, 25.0%** for the Classic 3-Tier baseline) at **0.35 ms vs. 4.72 ms median latency (13.5x speedup)**, generating self-contained bilingual 7-Card 4:5 HTML visual reports [`index.html` (Chinese)](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-authority-consent-eligibility-acp-3ds-fulfillment-ap2-jcs-tap-rfc9421-tau2-duplex-bench/index.html) and [`index.en.html` (English)](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-authority-consent-eligibility-acp-3ds-fulfillment-ap2-jcs-tap-rfc9421-tau2-duplex-bench/index.en.html).
- **中文**: `lab/ucp_authority_consent_eligibility_acp_3ds_fulfillment_ap2_jcs_tap_rfc9421_tau2_duplex_bench.py` 构建覆盖 Stage 80 四大护栏域的 16 个确定性评测场景（`AC-01..AC-16`）：AI-Native 护栏实现 **16/16 (100.0%)** 准确率与 **100.0%** 逆序扰动稳定性（对比经典三层架构基线 **4/16, 25.0%**），中位延迟 **0.35 ms vs. 4.72 ms（13.5x 加速）**，并生成中英双语自包含 7-Card 4:5 视觉评测报告 [`index.html`（中文）](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-authority-consent-eligibility-acp-3ds-fulfillment-ap2-jcs-tap-rfc9421-tau2-duplex-bench/index.html) 与 [`index.en.html`（English）](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-authority-consent-eligibility-acp-3ds-fulfillment-ap2-jcs-tap-rfc9421-tau2-duplex-bench/index.en.html)。

## 81. Stage 82 Guardrails: UCP Multi-Rendition Media / Method-Level Destinations / Object-Root `totals.json` / MCP Idempotency Contract / A2A `complete_in_progress` Freeze / Sanitized `order.payment` / Gift & Read-Only `ask` (`#690`, `#689`, `#684`, `#679`, `#675`, `#671`, `#670`, `#584`, `#578`, `#577`, `#566`, `#538`, `#508`), ACP Default Delivery Selection / Delegate Payment `401/500/503` & `virtual` Card / Citation Attribution & MCP Binding (`#198`, `#161`, `#149`, `#139`, `#132`, `#130`, `#125`), Coinbase x402 Pre-Sign Spend Policy / Request-Bound Payment ID / `attestseal` DID / Settlement Attestation Receipt (`SAR`) & `ERC-7710` (`#166`, `#165`, `#136`, `#46`, `#27`, `#22`, `#13`), and Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 Banking `close_bank_account_7392` Enum / Empty User-Sim Retry / Evaluator Provenance & TTS Concurrency (`#484`, `#478`, `#471`, `#467`, `#462`, `#457`)

### 81.1 UCP Multi-Rendition Media (`#690`, `#675`), Method-Level Destination Discrimination (`#689`), Object-Root `totals.json` (`#684`), MCP Idempotency Contract & A2A Freeze (`#679`, `#670`), Provisional `context.location` (`#671`), Sanitized `order.payment` (`#584`), Gift Line-Item & Rejection Codes (`#578`, `#577`), Canonical `keys[]` JWKS (`#566`), Read-Only `ask` (`#538`) & Recipient-Required Gift Checkout (`#508`)
- **English**: `verify_ucp_media_destinations_totals_idempotency_order_gift_and_ask_guard` in `lab/ucp_media_dest_totals_acp_default_deliv_mcp_x402_sar_erc7710_tau2_close_prov_guard.py` enforces UCP PRs `#690`/`#675` (`image`, `video`, `external_video`, `model_3d` media variants with non-empty `sources[]`), PR `#689` (method-level request destination discrimination without breaking deployed platforms, paired with explicit response `type`), PR `#684` (forbidding `RootArray` on `totals.json`), PRs `#679`/`#670` (returning `409`/`-32000` on mismatched MCP `idempotency-key` reuse and freezing A2A mutations during `complete_in_progress`), PR `#671` (`context.location` never auto-selecting delivery destination), PR `#584` (stripping `handler_id`, `credential`, and `selected` from `order.payment`), PRs `#578`/`#577` (`gift_line_item_id` linkage for zero-price promotional items and distinct `discount_code_usage_limit_reached`/`discount_code_stacking_conflict` codes), and PRs `#566`/`#538`/`#508` (single canonical `keys[]` JWKS, read-only `dev.ucp.common.ask`, and validated business-side recipient state with zero recipient PII leakage in `dev.ucp.shopping.gift`).
- **中文**: `verify_ucp_media_destinations_totals_idempotency_order_gift_and_ask_guard` 强制执行 UCP PR `#690`/`#675`（`image`/`video`/`external_video`/`model_3d` 多变体媒体及非空 `sources[]`）、PR `#689`（请求侧按方法区分目的地类型、响应侧显式标注 `type`）、PR `#684`（`totals.json` 禁用顶层数组 Schema）、PR `#679`/`#670`（MCP 结账 `idempotency-key` 跨载荷复用冲突必须返回 `409`/`-32000` 及 A2A `complete_in_progress` 写冻结）、PR `#671`（`context.location` 仅作可用性评估上下文，严禁自动选定收货目的地）、PR `#584`（`order.payment` 剥离 `handler_id`/`credential`/`selected` 采集态字段）、PR `#578`/`#577`（零价赠品行必须通过 `gift_line_item_id` 关联且区分次数耗尽与叠加冲突拒绝码）以及 PR `#566`/`#538`/`#508`（单一规范 `keys[]` JWKS、`dev.ucp.common.ask` 严格只读、礼品结账 `recipient_required` 必验且严禁携带收礼人 PII）。

### 81.2 ACP Default Delivery Option Pre-Selection (`#198`), Delegate Payment `401/500/503` & `virtual` Card (`#161`, `#132`), Content Citation Attribution (`#149`), MCP Transport Binding (`#139`), PaymentHandler `display_name` & Intent (`#130`) & Consolidated `AppliedDiscount` (`#125`)
- **English**: `verify_acp_default_delivery_delegate_errors_citation_and_mcp_binding_guard` enforces ACP PR `#198` (pre-selecting a valid default `selected_fulfillment_option_id` when `fulfillment_options[]` are returned on checkout create), PRs `#161`/`#132` (requiring boolean `PaymentMethodCard.virtual` and validating `401/500/503` and API-version `Error` enums), PR `#149` (`content_attribution` requiring `source_url` and `publisher_id` without overriding item prices), and PRs `#139`/`#130`/`#125` (JSON-RPC 2.0 MCP checkout tool binding, `PaymentHandler.display_name` + `authorize`/`capture` intent, and consolidating deprecated `DiscountDetail` into `AppliedDiscount`).
- **中文**: `verify_acp_default_delivery_delegate_errors_citation_and_mcp_binding_guard` 强制执行 ACP PR `#198`（创建结账返回 `fulfillment_options[]` 时必须预选有效默认配送项）、PR `#161`/`#132`（`PaymentMethodCard.virtual` 必填布尔值及 `401/500/503`/API 版本错误枚举校验）、PR `#149`（`content_attribution` 必填 `source_url` 与 `publisher_id` 且严禁篡改商品价格）以及 PR `#139`/`#130`/`#125`（MCP JSON-RPC 2.0 五大结账工具绑定、`PaymentHandler.display_name` 与 `authorize`/`capture` 意图校验、统一合并 `DiscountDetail` 至 `AppliedDiscount`）。

### 81.3 Coinbase x402 Pre-Sign `onPaymentRequested` Spend Policy (`#166`), Request-Bound Payment Identifier (`#165`), `attestseal` Ed25519 DID Attestation (`#136`), Settlement Attestation Receipt `SAR` (`#46`), `ERC-7710` Delegation Simulation (`#22`) & Facilitator Hook Isolation / CSPRNG Fee-Payer (`#13`)
- **English**: `verify_x402_mcp_policy_request_binding_attestseal_sar_erc7710_and_hook_guard` enforces Coinbase x402 PR `#166` (enforcing MCP spend limits, tool name, and network checks in code before signing rather than only in prompts), PR `#165` (binding payment identifiers to a normalized request+payment fingerprint and rejecting mismatched reuse with `409`), PR `#136` (`attestseal-trust-attestation` Ed25519 signature and independent authority DID cached `<= 24h`), PR `#46` (**Settlement Attestation Receipt (SAR)** cryptographically binding `tx_hash`, `request_digest`, and `delivered_resource_digest` to the actual delivered response body), and PRs `#22`/`#13` (`eth_call` simulation of `ERC-7710` `redeemDelegations`, isolating optional lifecycle hook exceptions during `verify`/`settle`, and CSPRNG `crypto/rand` SVM fee-payer selection).
- **中文**: `verify_x402_mcp_policy_request_binding_attestseal_sar_erc7710_and_hook_guard` 强制执行 Coinbase x402 PR `#166`（在钱包签名前通过代码层 `onPaymentRequested` 校验工具名、网络与金额上限，禁止仅靠提示词约束）、PR `#165`（支付标识符绑定规范化请求指纹，跨请求复用返回 `409`）、PR `#136`（`attestseal-trust-attestation` 独立权威 DID Ed25519 签名与 `<= 24h` 缓存校验）、PR `#46`（结算证明收据 `SAR` 将链上交易哈希、请求摘要与实际交付资源摘要三方签名绑定）以及 PR `#22`/`#13`（`ERC-7710` `redeemDelegations` 预模拟、隔离可选生命周期钩子异常、SVM 费率支付方强制使用 `crypto/rand` CSPRNG）。

### 81.4 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 Banking `close_bank_account_7392` Fixed Reason Enum (`#484`), Task 091 `persona` Key (`#478`), Empty Text-Simulator Retry (`#471`), Evaluator Provenance (`#467`), Multi-Customer Telecom DB-Safe Grading (`#462`) & Voice TTS Concurrency Bound (`#457`)
- **English**: `verify_tau2_banking_close_reason_persona_empty_retry_provenance_and_tts_guard` enforces Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#484` (constraining `close_bank_account_7392` `reason` to a fixed enum set to unblock tasks 065/068/069), PR `#478` (fixing the `persoca` key typo in banking task 091), PR `#471` (retrying empty text-mode user-simulator completions after tracking-only user tool calls instead of crashing as `infrastructure_error`), PR `#467` (recording evaluator provenance `evaluation_mode` and `state_source`), PR `#462` (requiring `DB` in multi-customer Telecom `C1001`/`C1002` reward bases so unrelated writes fail grading), and PR `#457` (bounding concurrent `synthesize_voice` TTS calls to the subscription cap to prevent ElevenLabs `429` dropped caller utterances).
- **中文**: `verify_tau2_banking_close_reason_persona_empty_retry_provenance_and_tts_guard` 强制执行 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#484`（将 `close_bank_account_7392` 关户原因约束为固定枚举集合，解决 T065/T068/T069 自由文本 0% 通过率缺陷）、PR `#478`（修复银行 T091 `persoca` 键名拼写错误）、PR `#471`（文本用户模拟器在触发仅跟踪工具后返回空消息时自动重试而非报错崩溃）、PR `#467`（记录评测器溯源元数据区分实时评测与参考回放）、PR `#462`（多客户电信任务强制包含 `DB` 奖励基准防止无关写库拿满分）以及 PR `#457`（限制 `synthesize_voice` 并发合成上限防止触发 ElevenLabs `429` 丢失语音轮次）。

## 83. Stage 84 Deep-Dive: UCP One-Time Shipping Destination Privacy & MCP Macro Alignment (`#435`/`#444`), ACP Account-to-Account Push Payments & 3DS Outcomes (`#30`/`#78`), Coinbase x402 EIP-3009 Revert Mapping & Google AP2 HNP `SessionAuthorization` (`#70`/`#74`) & Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 Duplicate Transaction `posting_sequence` & Multi-Card Fraud Closure (`#380`/`#413`)

### 83.1 UCP One-Time Use Shipping Destinations (`#435`) & MCP Schema Macro / Order Error Envelope Contract (`#444`)
- **English**: `validate_ucp_onetime_destination_and_mcp_binding_guard` in [`lab/ucp_onetime_dest_mcp_acp_a2a_3ds_x402_eip3009_ap2_hnp_tau2_dup_guard.py`](../../lab/ucp_onetime_dest_mcp_acp_a2a_3ds_x402_eip3009_ap2_hnp_tau2_dup_guard.py) enforces UCP PR `#435` (requiring `shipping_destination.one_time_use: true` to be echoed on the response and prohibiting persistence into the buyer's saved address book or profile/audience/marketing enrichment even when `buyer_consent.marketing == true`) and PR `#444` (aligning `checkout-mcp.md` and `order-mcp.md` with `cart-mcp.md` by rendering Input/Output schemas via `{{ schema_fields(...) }}`, removing duplicate per-operation `meta` lines, and documenting the `protocol-errors` envelope on `order-mcp.md`).
- **中文**: `validate_ucp_onetime_destination_and_mcp_binding_guard`（位于 [`lab/ucp_onetime_dest_mcp_acp_a2a_3ds_x402_eip3009_ap2_hnp_tau2_dup_guard.py`](../../lab/ucp_onetime_dest_mcp_acp_a2a_3ds_x402_eip3009_ap2_hnp_tau2_dup_guard.py)）强制执行 UCP PR `#435`（`shipping_destination.one_time_use: true` 必须在响应中原样回显，且即便 `buyer_consent.marketing == true` 也严禁将该礼品/代收地址写入买家地址簿或用于推荐/营销画像富化）与 PR `#444`（对齐 `checkout-mcp.md` 与 `order-mcp.md` 的 `{{ schema_fields(...) }}` 宏渲染、剔除逐操作重复声明的顶层 `meta` 字段并补齐 `order-mcp.md` 标准协议错误信封）。

### 83.2 ACP Account-to-Account (`dev.acp.account_to_account`) Push Payment Lifecycle (`#30`) & 3DS Authentication Outcomes (`#78`)
- **English**: `validate_acp_a2a_push_payment_and_3ds_outcome_guard` enforces ACP PR `#30` (`dev.acp.account_to_account` push bank transfer handler requiring `requires_delegate_payment: false`, `requires_pci_compliance: false`, handler-only `payment_data`, `complete_in_progress` session status while `bank_transfer_confirmation.status == "pending"`, complete `bank_instructions` with `account_address`/`bank_name`/`reference`/`expires_at`, and timely `received_at <= expires_at` before fulfillment release) and PR `#78` (blocking settlement on `authentication_result.outcome == "denied"` and requiring `three_ds_cryptogram` + valid `electronic_commerce_indicator` on frictionless `outcome == "attempt_acknowledged"`).
- **中文**: `validate_acp_a2a_push_payment_and_3ds_outcome_guard` 强制执行 ACP PR `#30`（`dev.acp.account_to_account` 推式银行转账处理器：强制 `requires_delegate_payment: false`、`requires_pci_compliance: false`、纯 `handler_id` 载荷、待到账期间保持 `complete_in_progress` 状态并阻断提前发货、必填 `bank_instructions` 四要素及 `received_at <= expires_at` 到账时效校验）与 PR `#78`（3DS 认证 `outcome == "denied"` 严禁清算扣款，无感摩擦流 `outcome == "attempt_acknowledged"` 必填非空 `three_ds_cryptogram` 与合法 `electronic_commerce_indicator`）。

### 83.3 Coinbase x402 EIP-3009 Simulation vs. Revert Error Mapping (`#70`) & Google AP2 Human-Not-Present `SessionAuthorization` / `SpendingRuleSet` (`#74`)
- **English**: `validate_x402_eip3009_error_and_ap2_hnp_session_guard` enforces Coinbase x402 PR `#70` (separating `verifyEIP3009` RPC simulation failures as `ErrEip3009SimulationFailed` from `ErrInvalidPayload`, and mapping `ExecuteTransferWithAuthorization` contract revert reasons via `parseEIP3009TransferError` into `ErrValidBeforeExpired`, `ErrValidAfterInFuture`, `ErrNonceAlreadyUsed`, `ErrInsufficientBalance`, and `ErrInvalidSignature`) and Google AP2 PR `#74` (validating the 3-tier `User -> Agent DID -> SessionAuthorization` hierarchy with active non-expired session keys and priority-ordered `SpendingRuleSet` `FrequencyConstraint` / `CategoryConstraint` / `AmountConstraint` under `all` or `any` evaluation modes).
- **中文**: `validate_x402_eip3009_error_and_ap2_hnp_session_guard` 强制执行 Coinbase x402 PR `#70`（在 `verifyEIP3009` 中将 RPC 模拟失败映射为 `ErrEip3009SimulationFailed` 而非误报 `ErrInvalidPayload`，并在 `settleEIP3009` 中通过 `parseEIP3009TransferError` 精确映射过期、未生效、Nonce 已用、余额不足与签名无效五类 EIP-3009 合约回滚码）与 Google AP2 PR `#74`（校验人不在场 HNP 场景的 `User -> Agent DID -> SessionAuthorization` 三层身份授权、会话公钥有效窗及按优先级排序的 `SpendingRuleSet` 频次/类目/金额规则集）。

### 83.4 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 Banking Duplicate Transaction `posting_sequence` Disambiguation (`#380`) & Multi-Card Confirmed Fraud Remediation (`#413`)
- **English**: `validate_tau2_duplicate_posting_seq_and_multicard_fraud_guard` enforces Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#380` (requiring unique positive integer `posting_sequence` values across duplicate posted debit-card transactions in `task_083`/`task_084` and requiring the agent to dispute the earliest transaction with `posting_sequence == 1`) and PR `#413` (requiring `close_debit_card_4721(..., reason="fraud_suspected")` across every fraud-confirmed card in multi-card dispute trajectories such as `task_082`'s Green Fee-Free card `dbc_mc47a2b9e1_gff` so zero confirmed-fraud cards remain `ACTIVE`).
- **中文**: `validate_tau2_duplicate_posting_seq_and_multicard_fraud_guard` 强制执行 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#380`（在 `task_083`/`task_084` 重复入账借记卡交易组中强制提供唯一正整数 `posting_sequence`，并要求争议最早入账的 `posting_sequence == 1` 交易）与 PR `#413`（在 `task_082` 等跨卡复合欺诈争议中，要求对所有确认欺诈的借记卡如绿卡 `dbc_mc47a2b9e1_gff` 执行 `close_debit_card_4721(..., reason="fraud_suspected")`，严禁遗留任何确认欺诈卡片处于 `ACTIVE` 状态）。
